const AppIcon = ({ appId, size = 'md' }) => {
  const dimensions = size === 'lg' ? 'w-16 h-16 rounded-[1.25rem]' : size === 'sm' ? 'w-10 h-10 rounded-xl' : 'w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl';
  const iconDim = size === 'lg' ? 'w-8 h-8' : size === 'sm' ? 'w-5 h-5' : 'w-7 h-7';
  
  switch (appId) {
    case 'line': return (<div className={`${dimensions} shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden`}><img src="https://play-lh.googleusercontent.com/HhsyrXZLcFtoOs2_LF17yvxwDbimYaioLIhLdEO3AvZlemvM2iCzBTX27jPzd19A9vvGR-Mg3Cy9euJ22LVdgQ" alt="LINE" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /></div>);
    case 'facebook': return (<div className={`${dimensions} shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden`}><img src="https://play-lh.googleusercontent.com/KCMTYuiTrKom4Vyf0G4foetVOwhKWzNbHWumV73IXexAIy5TTgZipL52WTt8ICL-oIo" alt="Facebook" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /></div>);
    case 'instagram': return (<div className={`${dimensions} shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden`}><img src="https://store-images.s-microsoft.com/image/apps.43327.13510798887167234.cadff69d-8229-427b-a7da-21dbaf80bd81.79b8f512-1b22-45d6-9495-881485e3a87e" alt="Instagram" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /></div>);
    case 'tiktok': return (<div className={`${dimensions} shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden`}><img src="https://play-lh.googleusercontent.com/t5yNYUzJYyOzLtXXGETvuGfgQEkMdGytKr5t35WMZlva0FKgOEl7chJSrzQ848lm-jUirB2saX2rbqrIJffr=w240-h480-rw" alt="TikTok" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /></div>);
    case 'youtube': return (<div className={`${dimensions} bg-[#FF0000] shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center shrink-0`}><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" className={`${iconDim} text-white`} fill="currentColor"><path d="M23.498 6.186a3.016 3.016 0 0 0-2.122-2.136C19.505 3.545 12 3.545 12 3.545s-7.505 0-9.377.505A3.017 3.017 0 0 0 .502 6.186C0 8.07 0 12 0 12s0 3.93.502 5.814a3.016 3.016 0 0 0 2.122 2.136c1.871.505 9.376.505 9.376.505s7.505 0 9.377-.505a3.015 3.015 0 0 0 2.122-2.136C24 15.93 24 12 24 12s0-3.93-.502-5.814zM9.545 15.568V8.432L15.818 12l-6.273 3.568z"/></svg></div>);

// ==========================================
// AUTH SCREEN
// ==========================================
function PlatformAuth({ onLogin }) {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const handleLogin = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setTimeout(() => { setIsLoading(false); onLogin(); }, 1200);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-pink-50/30 flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4 relative overflow-hidden" style={{ fontFamily: "'Anuphan', sans-serif" }}>
      <style dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{__html: `@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Anuphan:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap');`}} />
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-full h-full overflow-hidden pointer-events-none z-0">
        <div className="absolute -top-[20%] -right-[10%] w-[70%] h-[70%] bg-pink-400/15 blur-[120px] rounded-full animate-pulse"></div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-[10%] -left-[10%] w-[50%] h-[50%] bg-violet-400/15 blur-[100px] rounded-full animate-pulse" style={{ animationDelay: '1s' }}></div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col items-center animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-500 z-10 hover:-translate-y-2 transition-transform">
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl shadow-pink-500/20 mb-4 flex justify-center items-center border-[3px] border-pink-100">
           <img src="https://i.postimg.cc/SxfdKzYs/AIVA-Logo-01.png" alt="AIVA Logo" className="h-24 w-24 object-contain" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-2xl rounded-[3rem] shadow-[0_20px_60px_-15px_rgba(236,72,153,0.2)] border-2 border-white overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500 p-8 z-10">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 mb-6 text-center">เข้าสู่ระบบการจัดการ</h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
               <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">อีเมลพนักงาน (Email)</label>
               <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-600 bg-violet-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-violet-100">ทีมงาน / แอดมิน</span>
            </div>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="w-5 h-5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
              <input type="email" placeholder="staff@company.com" defaultValue="admin@globaltech.com" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-100 text-slate-900 rounded-2xl pl-10 pr-4 py-3.5 text-sm focus:ring-4 focus:ring-pink-500/20 focus:border-pink-400 transition-all outline-none" required />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 my-2">
            <div className="h-px bg-slate-200 flex-1"></div>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">หรือ</span>
            <div className="h-px bg-slate-200 flex-1"></div>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
               <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">รหัสลูกค้า (Customer ID)</label>
               <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-pink-600 bg-pink-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-pink-100">เจ้าของกิจการ (Owner)</span>
            </div>
            <div className="relative">
              <Hash className="w-5 h-5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
              <input type="text" placeholder="CXXXXXX" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-100 text-slate-900 rounded-2xl pl-10 pr-4 py-3.5 text-sm focus:ring-4 focus:ring-pink-500/20 focus:border-pink-400 transition-all outline-none uppercase" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5 mt-4">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">รหัสผ่าน</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Lock className="w-5 h-5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
              <input type="password" placeholder="••••••••" defaultValue="password" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-100 text-slate-900 rounded-2xl pl-10 pr-4 py-3.5 text-sm focus:ring-4 focus:ring-pink-500/20 focus:border-pink-400 transition-all outline-none" required />
            </div>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" disabled={isLoading} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-violet-500 hover:from-pink-400 hover:to-violet-400 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-2xl transition-all shadow-lg shadow-pink-500/30 flex justify-center items-center gap-2 mt-6 transform hover:-translate-y-1 active:scale-95">
            {isLoading ? <div className="w-5 h-5 border-4 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin"></div> : 'เข้าสู่ระบบ AIVA Platform ✨'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

    );
  };

  const handlePublishPost = () => {
    if (selectedPlatforms.length === 0) {
      alert('กรุณาเลือกช่องทางอย่างน้อย 1 ช่องทาง');
      return;
    }
    setIsPublishing(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setIsPublishing(false);
      setPublishSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setPublishSuccess(false), 3000);
    }, 2000);

  const [growthActionSuccess, setGrowthActionSuccess] = useState(false);

  const [isGeneratingMessage, setIsGeneratingMessage] = useState(false);

  const [showFeedbackSuccess, setShowFeedbackSuccess] = useState(false);

  const [teamMembers, setTeamMembers] = useState([
    { id: 'u1', name: 'สมชาย ใจดี', email: 'owner@globaltech.com', role: 'OWNER', status: 'Active', branch: 'All Branches' }
  ]);
  const [showAddMember, setShowAddMember] = useState(false);
  const [newMember, setNewMember] = useState({ name: '', email: '', role: 'ADMIN', branch: 'HQ' });

  // Branch Management State
  const [branchData, setBranchData] = useState(MOCK_BRANCHES);
  const [showAddBranch, setShowAddBranch] = useState(false);
  const [newBranch, setNewBranch] = useState({ name: '', manager: '', status: 'Active', customAi: false });

  // Translation Helper
  const currentLang = LANGUAGES[langIndex];
  const t = (key) => TRANSLATIONS[currentLang][key] || key;
  const cycleLanguage = () => setLangIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % LANGUAGES.length);

  // Computed Values
  const tokenPercentage = (MOCK_STATS.tokensUsed / MOCK_STATS.tokenLimit) * 100;
  const isSidebarVisible = isSidebarPinned || isSidebarHovered;

  const isProOrAbove = currentPlan === 'Pro' || currentPlan === 'Advanced';
  const isAdvancedOnly = currentPlan === 'Advanced';

  // Handlers
  const toggleLeadAi = (id) => setLeadScores(leadScores.map(lead => lead.id === id ? { ...lead, aiEnabled: !lead.aiEnabled } : lead));
  const toggleLostRevenueAuto = (id) => setLostRevenues(lostRevenues.map(item => item.id === id ? { ...item, autoEnabled: !item.autoEnabled } : item));
  
  const handleSaveSettings = () => {
    setSettingsSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSettingsSaved(false), 2000); 
  };
  const handleAddMember = () => {
    if(!newMember.name || !newMember.email) return;
    const added = { ...newMember, id: Date.now().toString(), status: 'Pending' };
    setTeamMembers([...teamMembers, added]);
    setShowAddMember(false);
    setNewMember({ name: '', email: '', role: 'ADMIN', branch: 'HQ' });
  };
  const handleRemoveMember = (id) => setTeamMembers(teamMembers.filter(m => m.id !== id));
  
  const handleAddBranch = () => {
    if(!newBranch.name) return;
    const added = { ...newBranch, id: Date.now().toString(), chats: 0, revenue: 0 };

    setShowAddBranch(false);
    setNewBranch({ name: '', manager: '', status: 'Active', customAi: false });
  };

  const handleAddLead = () => {
    if(!newLead.name) return;
    const added = { ...newLead, id: Date.now(), date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('th-TH') };
    setLeadsData([added, ...leadsData]);
    setShowAddLead(false);
    setNewLead({ name: '', contact: '', intent: '', status: 'New' });
  };
  
  const handleGenerateFollowUpMessage = () => {
    setIsGeneratingMessage(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setNewRule(prev => ({

      }));
      setIsGeneratingMessage(false);
    }, 1500);
  };

  const handleCreateRule = () => {
    if(!newRule.name || !newRule.message) return;
    setShowCreateRule(false);
    setNewRule({ name: '', delay: '24h', smartTiming: false, message: '', includeCoupon: false, discountPercentage: 5 });
  };
  const handleAddDeal = () => {
    if(!newDeal.name) return;
    const added = { ...newDeal, id: Date.now(), time: 'เพิ่งเพิ่ม', value: Number(newDeal.value) || 0 };
    setPipelineData([added, ...pipelineData]);
    setShowAddDeal(false);
    setNewDeal({ name: '', intent: '', score: 'Warm', stage: 'New Leads', value: '' });
  };
  const handleGrowthAction = () => {
    setGrowthActionLoading(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setGrowthActionLoading(false);
      setGrowthActionSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setGrowthActionSuccess(false), 3000);
    }, 1500);
  };
  const handleConnect = (appId) => { 
    if (connectedApps.length >= maxChannels) {
      alert(`คุณใช้โควต้าเชื่อมต่อครบแล้ว (${maxChannels} ช่องทาง) กรุณาอัปเกรดแพ็กเกจ`);
      return;
    }
    setConnectingApp(appId); setIsAuthorizing(false); 
  };
  const handleAuthorize = () => {
    setIsAuthorizing(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setConnectedApps([...connectedApps, connectingApp]);
      setConnectingApp(null);
      setIsAuthorizing(false);
    }, 2000);
  };
  const handleDisconnect = (appId) => {
    setConnectedApps(connectedApps.filter(id => id !== appId));
    setManagingApp(null);
    if (inboxPlatformFilter === PLATFORM_MAP[appId]) setInboxPlatformFilter('All');
    if (replyPlatformFilter === PLATFORM_MAP[appId]) setReplyPlatformFilter('All');
  };

  const handleGenerateContent = () => {
    if (!contentInput) return;
    setIsGenerating(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setGeneratedContent(`✨ ไอเท็มสุดคิวท์มาแล้ว! ใครกำลังตามหา ${contentInput.substring(0,20)}... ต้องจัดเลยน้าาา 💖 \n\nคุณภาพดี๊ดี ใช้แล้วปังแน่นอน ช้าหมดอดน้าา 🛍️\n\n👉 ทักแชทสั่งซื้อได้เลยค่ะ`);
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }, 1500);
  };

  const handleSubmitFeedback = () => {
    if (!feedbackForm.title.trim() || !feedbackForm.description.trim()) return;
    setIsSubmittingFeedback(true);
    setTimeout(() => {

      setFeedbackList([newItem, ...feedbackList]);
      setFeedbackForm({ type: 'feature', title: '', description: '' });
      setIsSubmittingFeedback(false);
      setShowFeedbackSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setShowFeedbackSuccess(false), 5000);
    }, 1500);
  };

  // Auto scroll chat
  useEffect(() => {
    if (chatEndRef.current) {
      chatEndRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  }, [selectedChat, chatMode, chats, isAiTyping]);

  // Auth Guard
  if (!isAuthenticated) return <PlatformAuth onLogin={() => setIsAuthenticated(true)} />;

  const handleLogout = () => setIsAuthenticated(false);

  // Send Manual Admin Message Handler
  const handleSendAdminReply = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!adminReplyText.trim()) return;
    const now = new Date();
    const timeString = now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    
    const newMsg = {
      sender: 'ai', // sending as brand
      text: adminReplyText,
      time: timeString
    };

    setChats(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [selectedChat]: [...(prev[selectedChat] || []), newMsg]
    }));
    setAdminReplyText('');
    setShowSlashCommands(false);
  };

  const handleAdminTextChange = (e) => {
    setAdminReplyText(e.target.value);
    if (e.target.value.endsWith('/')) {
      setShowSlashCommands(true);
    } else {
      setShowSlashCommands(false);
    }
  };

  const insertSlashCommand = (text) => {
    setAdminReplyText(adminReplyText.slice(0, -1) + text);
    setShowSlashCommands(false);
  };

  const handleAttachFile = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (file) {
      const now = new Date();
      const timeString = now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
      
      const imageUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      
      const newMsg = {
        sender: 'ai', // sending as brand
        text: '',
        image: imageUrl,
        time: timeString
      };

      setChats(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        [selectedChat]: [...(prev[selectedChat] || []), newMsg]
      }));
      
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  // Simulator helper to append client message and invoke AIVA auto-response with media
  const simulateCustomerMessage = (type) => {
    const now = new Date();
    const timeString = now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    
    let clientMessageText = '';
    let aiResponseText = '';
    let aiImage = null;
    let aiButtons = null;

    if (type === 'size') {

      aiResponseText = 'ตารางไซส์มาตรฐานสำหรับเดรสและเสื้อผ้าแฟชั่นหน้าร้อน (Summer Standard) ค่ะคุณลูกค้า ☀️ แนะนำให้อิงสัดส่วนรอบอก (Bust) เป็นหลักนะคะ หากมีข้อสงสัยเรื่องรอบเอวหรือความกว้างบ่า ทักถามแอดมินคนนี้ได้เลยค่า 💕';
      aiImage = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1543087903-1ac2ec7aa8c5?w=600&auto=format&fit=crop&q=80'; 
      aiButtons = ['สอบถามรอบอก 34"', 'สอบถามรอบอก 36"', 'ต้องการไซส์พิเศษ'];
    } else if (type === 'promo') {
      clientMessageText = 'เดือนนี้มีโปรโมชั่นพิเศษอะไรบ้างคะ? อยากได้แบนเนอร์ไปแชร์ต่อค่ะ';
      aiResponseText = 'แคมเปญโปรโมชั่นต้อนรับหน้าร้อน June Summer Grand Sale มาแล้วค่ะคุณลูกค้า! 🎉\n🛍️ เพียงช้อปครบ 2,500 บาทขึ้นไป รับส่วนลดทันที 15% พร้อมบริการจัดส่งฟรี และรับโค้ดส่วนลดเพิ่มเติมสำหรับการสั่งซื้อครั้งถัดไปด้วยค่ะ!';
      aiImage = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1472851294608-062f824d296e?w=600&auto=format&fit=crop&q=80'; 
      aiButtons = ['เก็บโค้ดส่วนลด 15%', 'ขอดูสินค้าโปรโมชั่น'];
    } else if (type === 'delivery') {
      clientMessageText = 'ทางร้านส่งสินค้าผ่านขนส่งเจ้าไหนเหรอคะ?';
      aiResponseText = 'ทางร้านใช้บริการจัดส่งด่วนพิเศษของ Flash Express และ Kerry Express เป็นหลักค่ะบอส 📦 มั่นใจได้เลยว่าสินค้าจะถูกแพ็คอย่างดีและส่งตรงถึงมือคุณอย่างรวดเร็วแน่นอนค่ะ';
    }

    const clientMsg = { sender: 'user', text: clientMessageText, time: timeString };

    setChats(prev => ({ ...prev, [selectedChat]: [...(prev[selectedChat] || []), clientMsg] }));
    setIsAiTyping(true);

    setTimeout(() => {
      setIsAiTyping(false);
      const aiMsg = { sender: 'ai', text: aiResponseText, image: aiImage, buttons: aiButtons, time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) };
      setChats(prev => ({ ...prev, [selectedChat]: [...(prev[selectedChat] || []), aiMsg] }));
    }, 1200);
  };

                          <span className={`absolute bottom-0 text-[10px] font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{days[i]}</span>
                        </div>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className={`rounded-2xl p-0 flex flex-col overflow-hidden glass-card ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'}`}>
                  <div className={`p-5 border-b flex justify-between items-center ${isDarkMode ? 'border-slate-700' : 'border-slate-100'}`}>
                    <h2 className={`text-base font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}><AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500"/> {t('missingKnowledge')}</h2>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-2">
                    {[
                      { query: 'มีเก็บเงินปลายทางไหมคะ?', count: 124, status: 'รอการสอน' },
                      { query: 'ส่งของผ่านขนส่งอะไรคะ?', count: 85, status: 'รอการสอน' },
                      { query: 'ขอวิธีใช้สินค้าแบบวิดีโอ', count: 42, status: 'รอการสอน' },
                    ].map((item, i) => (
                      <div key={i} className={`p-3 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors border-b last:border-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700/50 border-slate-700/50' : 'hover:bg-slate-50 border-slate-50'}`}>
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                          <p className={`text-xs font-bold truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>"{item.query}"</p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                           <span className={`text-[9px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400' : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-600'}`}>ถาม {item.count} ครั้ง</span>
                           <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('knowledge')} className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"><Plus className="w-3 h-3"/> เพิ่มคำตอบให้ AI</button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB: KNOWLEDGE BASE */}
          {activeTab === 'knowledge' && (
            <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto animate-in fade-in duration-300 flex flex-col h-full">
              <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 shrink-0">
                <div>
                  <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}><BookOpen className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" /> {t('knowledge')}</h2>
                  <p className={`text-sm mt-1 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{t('knowledgeDesc')}</p>
                </div>
                <div className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-center border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-900/30 border-indigo-500/30' : 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-100'}`}>
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-0.5">{t('trainedData')}</p>
                  <p className={`text-lg font-black ${isDarkMode ? 'text-indigo-300' : 'text-indigo-900'}`}>{MOCK_KNOWLEDGE.length} <span className={`text-sm font-medium ${isDarkMode ? 'text-indigo-400' : 'text-indigo-700'}`}>{t('items')}</span></p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8 shrink-0">
                <button className={`hover:border-indigo-400 hover:shadow-lg transition-all p-6 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center group cursor-pointer h-40 border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'}`}>
                  <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-3 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400' : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-500'}`}><FileText className="w-6 h-6" /></div>
                  <h3 className={`font-bold text-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{t('uploadPdf')}</h3>
                  <p className={`text-xs mt-1 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>คู่มือ, แคตตาล็อก, นโยบายบริษัท</p>
                </button>
                <button className={`hover:border-indigo-400 hover:shadow-lg transition-all p-6 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center group cursor-pointer h-40 border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'}`}>
                  <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-3 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400' : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-500'}`}><LinkIcon className="w-6 h-6" /></div>
                  <h3 className={`font-bold text-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{t('extractUrl')}</h3>
                  <p className={`text-xs mt-1 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>หน้า FAQ, หน้าสินค้าบนเว็บ</p>
                </button>
                <button className={`hover:border-indigo-400 hover:shadow-lg transition-all p-6 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center group cursor-pointer h-40 border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'}`}>
                  <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-3 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400' : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-500'}`}><MessageSquare className="w-6 h-6" /></div>
                  <h3 className={`font-bold text-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{t('addText')}</h3>
                  <p className={`text-xs mt-1 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>โปรโมชั่นด่วน, ถาม-ตอบสั้นๆ</p>
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className={`rounded-2xl flex flex-col flex-1 overflow-hidden border shadow-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'} min-h-0`}>
                <div className={`p-4 border-b flex justify-between items-center shrink-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-700' : 'bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-100'}`}>
                  <h3 className={`font-bold text-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{t('aiBrain')}</h3>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                    <input type="text" placeholder={t('searchFile')} className={`text-sm rounded-lg pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 w-64 border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-800'}`} />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="overflow-auto custom-scrollbar flex-1 p-0">
                  <table className="w-full text-left text-sm table-fixed min-w-[700px]">
                    <thead className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold border-b sticky top-0 z-10 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white text-slate-500 border-slate-100'}`}>
                      <tr>
                        <th className="px-4 py-3 w-[45%]">{t('nameSource')}</th>
                        <th className="px-4 py-3 w-[15%] text-center">{t('size')}</th>
                        <th className="px-4 py-3 w-[15%] text-center">{t('updatedDate')}</th>
                        <th className="px-4 py-3 w-[10%] text-center">Tokens</th>
                        <th className="px-4 py-3 w-[15%] text-center">{t('status')}</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className={`divide-y ${isDarkMode ? 'divide-slate-700/50' : 'divide-slate-100'}`}>
                      {MOCK_KNOWLEDGE.map((item) => (
                        <tr key={item.id} className={`transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700/30' : 'hover:bg-slate-50'}`}>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 overflow-hidden">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                              <div className={`p-2 rounded-lg shrink-0 ${
                                item.type === 'pdf' ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-rose-500/20' : 'bg-rose-50') : 
                                item.type === 'url' ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-blue-500/20' : 'bg-blue-50') : 
                                (isDarkMode ? 'bg-amber-500/20' : 'bg-amber-50')
                              }`}>
                                {item.type === 'pdf' && <FileText className={`w-4 h-4 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-rose-500'}`} />}
                                {item.type === 'url' && <LinkIcon className={`w-4 h-4 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-blue-400' : 'text-blue-500'}`} />}
                                {item.type === 'text' && <MessageSquare className={`w-4 h-4 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-amber-500'}`} />}
                              </div>
                              <span className={`font-bold truncate w-full block ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>{item.name}</span>
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td className={`px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{item.size}</td>
                          <td className={`px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{item.date}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                            <span className={`font-mono text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'}`}>{item.tokens}</span>
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                            {item.status === 'Trained' ? (
                              <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/20' : 'text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200'}`}>
                                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3"/> เรียนรู้แล้ว
                              </span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/20' : 'text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 border-amber-200'}`}>
                                <RefreshCw className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin"/> Training
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB: INBOX */}
          {activeTab === 'inbox' && (
            <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto h-full flex flex-col animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              
              <div className="mb-4 shrink-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>
                    <MessageSquare className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" /> {t('inbox')}
                  </h2>
                </div>
                <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 p-1.5 rounded-xl border shadow-sm shrink-0 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'}`}>
                  {['All', ...connectedApps.map(id => PLATFORM_MAP[id])].map(platform => (
                    <button
                      key={platform}
                      onClick={() => setInboxPlatformFilter(platform)}
                      className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors ${
                        inboxPlatformFilter === platform
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                          : (isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-50')
                      }`}
                    >
                      {platform === 'All' ? t('allChannels') : platform}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className={`flex-1 flex rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border shadow-sm min-h-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'}`}>
                
                <div className={`w-80 flex flex-col border-r shrink-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'border-slate-700' : 'border-slate-100'}`}>
                  <div className={`p-4 border-b flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-100 bg-slate-50'}`}>
                    <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                    <input type="text" placeholder={t('searchCustomer')} className={`w-full bg-transparent border-none text-sm outline-none ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-200 placeholder:text-slate-500' : 'text-slate-800'}`} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-2 space-y-1">
                    {(() => {
                      const visibleChats = MOCK_INBOX_LIST.filter(chat => {
                        const platformId = Object.keys(PLATFORM_MAP).find(k => PLATFORM_MAP[k] === chat.platform);
                        const isConnected = connectedApps.includes(platformId);
                        if (!isConnected) return false;
                        return inboxPlatformFilter === 'All' || chat.platform === inboxPlatformFilter;
                      });

                      if (visibleChats.length === 0) {
                        return (
                          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-slate-400 py-10 opacity-70">
                            <MessageSquare className="w-8 h-8 mb-2" />
                            <p className="text-xs font-bold">ไม่มีข้อความในช่องทางนี้</p>
                          </div>
                        );
                      }

                      return visibleChats.map((chat) => (
                        <div 
                          key={chat.id} 
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedChat(chat.id);
                            if(chat.status === 'Handover') setChatMode('human');
                            else setChatMode('ai');
                          }}
                          className={`p-3 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors border ${
                            selectedChat === chat.id 
                              ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500/20 border-indigo-500/30' : 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-100') 
                              : (isDarkMode ? 'border-transparent hover:bg-slate-700/50' : 'border-transparent hover:bg-slate-50')
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-1">
                            <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
                              <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white ${
                                chat.platform === 'Line OA' ? 'bg-[#00B900]' : 
                                chat.platform === 'Facebook' ? 'bg-[#0084FF]' : 
                                chat.platform === 'Instagram' ? 'bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500 to-purple-600' : 
                                chat.platform === 'TikTok Shop' ? 'bg-slate-900' :
                                chat.platform === 'Lazada' ? 'bg-[#0F146D]' :
                                'bg-slate-800'
                              }`}>
                                {chat.user.charAt(0)}
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex flex-col">
                                {chat.user}
                                {chat.tags && (
                                  <div className="flex gap-1 mt-1">
                                    {chat.tags.map((tag, i) => (
                                      <span key={i} className={`text-[8px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold ${tag === 'VIP' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-600' : tag === 'โอนไว' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600' : isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'}`}>
                                        {tag}
                                      </span>
                                    ))}
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium">{chat.time}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="pl-10 mt-1">
                            <p className={`text-xs truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-600'}`}>
                              {chat.status === 'AI Replied' ? <Bot className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1 text-emerald-500"/> : <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1 text-rose-500"/>}
                              "{chat.query}"
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ));
                    })()}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className={`flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900/50' : 'bg-slate-50/50'}`}>
                  {/* Top Bar for Selected Chat */}
                  <div className={`h-16 px-6 border-b flex items-center justify-between shrink-0 backdrop-blur-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800/80 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white/80 border-slate-100'}`}>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-500'}`}>
                        {MOCK_INBOX_LIST.find(c => c.id === selectedChat)?.user.charAt(0)}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <h3 className={`font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{MOCK_INBOX_LIST.find(c => c.id === selectedChat)?.user}</h3>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">ผ่านช่องทาง {MOCK_INBOX_LIST.find(c => c.id === selectedChat)?.platform}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className={`flex items-center p-1 rounded-xl border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700' : 'bg-slate-100 border-slate-200'}`}>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => setChatMode('ai')}
                        className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${chatMode === 'ai' ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700 text-emerald-400 shadow-sm' : 'bg-white text-emerald-600 shadow-sm') : (isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600')}`}
                      >
                        <Bot className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {t('aiHandling')}
                      </button>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => setChatMode('human')}
                        className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${chatMode === 'human' ? 'bg-rose-500 text-white shadow-sm' : (isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600')}`}
                      >
                        <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {t('humanHandling')}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Customer Simulator Quick Bar (FOR DEMO PURPOSE) */}
                  <div className={`px-6 py-2.5 border-b flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 shrink-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800/40 border-slate-700/60' : 'bg-indigo-50/50 border-slate-100'}`}>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${isDarkMode ? 'text-indigo-400' : 'text-indigo-600'}`}>🛠️ เครื่องมือจำลองแชทลูกค้า (Customer Simulator):</span>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => simulateCustomerMessage('size')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border flex items-center gap-1 transition-all ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border-slate-700 hover:border-indigo-500/50' : 'bg-white hover:bg-indigo-50 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-200'}`}
                      >
                        <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500" />
                        ถามตารางไซส์ (Size Chart)
                      </button>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => simulateCustomerMessage('promo')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border flex items-center gap-1 transition-all ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border-slate-700 hover:border-indigo-500/50' : 'bg-white hover:bg-indigo-50 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-200'}`}
                      >
                        <Tag className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" />
                        ถามโปรโมชั่น (Promo Image)
                      </button>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => simulateCustomerMessage('delivery')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border flex items-center gap-1 transition-all ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border-slate-700 hover:border-indigo-500/50' : 'bg-white hover:bg-indigo-50 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-200'}`}
                      >
                        <Store className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500" />
                        ถามเรื่องขนส่ง (Text FAQ)
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Chat Messages Log */}
                  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-4 custom-scrollbar">
                    <div className="text-center mb-6">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-500'}`}>วันนี้ 10:00 AM</span>
                    </div>

                    {chats[selectedChat]?.map((msg, idx) => (
                      <div key={idx} className={`flex items-start gap-3 max-w-[90%] ${msg.sender === 'user' ? 'mr-auto' : 'ml-auto flex-row-reverse'}`}>
                        <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-bold text-xs ${
                          msg.sender === 'user' 
                            ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-500') 
                            : (isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/30' : 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 border border-indigo-200')
                        }`}>
                          {msg.sender === 'user' ? 'C' : <Bot className="w-4 h-4"/>}
                        </div>
                        <div className={`flex flex-col gap-2 w-full ${msg.sender === 'user' ? 'items-start' : 'items-end'}`}>
                          {/* Text Message Bubble */}
                          {msg.text && (
                            <div className={`px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm shadow-sm whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed max-w-[85%] ${
                              msg.sender === 'user' 
                                ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-100 rounded-tl-sm' : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border border-slate-100 rounded-tl-sm') 
                                : (isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500 text-white rounded-tr-sm' : 'bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-tr-sm')
                            }`}>
                              {msg.text}
                            </div>
                          )}

                          {/* Image Attachment (For Rich Media) */}
                          {msg.image && (
                            <div className={`rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-md max-w-[320px] border relative group ${isDarkMode ? 'border-slate-700' : 'border-slate-200'} ${msg.sender === 'user' ? 'rounded-tl-sm' : 'rounded-tr-sm'}`}>
                              <img src={msg.image} alt="rich attachment" className="w-full h-auto object-cover max-h-60" />
                              {msg.isSlip ? (
                                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-emerald-500/20 backdrop-blur-[2px] flex items-center justify-center animate-in fade-in duration-500">
                                  <div className="bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xl">
                                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4"/> สลิปถูกต้อง (AIVA Verified)
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              ) : (
                                <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-md px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1">
                                  <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-400" /> จากคลังความรู้ AI
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          )}

                          {/* Quick Reply Action Buttons */}
                          {msg.buttons && (
                            <div className={`flex flex-wrap gap-2 ${msg.sender === 'user' ? 'justify-start' : 'justify-end'}`}>
                              {msg.buttons.map((btn, i) => (
                                <button key={i} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-colors shadow-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-indigo-500/40 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-indigo-200 text-indigo-700 hover:bg-indigo-50'}`}>
                                  {btn}
                                </button>
                              ))}
                            </div>
                          )}

                          {/* Product Carousel Flex Message */}
                          {msg.carousel && (
                            <div className={`flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar pb-2 max-w-[320px] ${msg.sender === 'user' ? 'flex-row' : 'flex-row-reverse'}`}>
                              {msg.carousel.map((item, i) => (
                                <div key={i} className={`flex-shrink-0 w-36 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border shadow-sm flex flex-col ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'}`}>
                                  <img src={item.image} alt={item.title} className="w-full h-36 object-cover" />
                                  <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1">
                                    <p className={`text-xs font-bold truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{item.title}</p>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-indigo-500 font-black mt-0.5 mb-2">{item.price}</p>
                                    <button className={`w-full py-1.5 mt-auto rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500 text-white hover:bg-indigo-400' : 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 hover:bg-indigo-100'}`}>ดูรายละเอียด</button>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              ))}
                            </div>
                          )}

                          <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 px-1">{msg.sender === 'ai' && <Bot className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-0.5 opacity-50"/>}{msg.time}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}

                    {/* AI Simulated Typing Indicator */}
                    {isAiTyping && (
                      <div className="flex items-start gap-3 max-w-[90%] ml-auto flex-row-reverse animate-pulse">
                        <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-bold text-xs ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/30' : 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 border border-indigo-200'}`}>
                          <Bot className="w-4 h-4"/>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 items-end">
                          <div className={`px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs shadow-sm bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-tr-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-500`}>
                            <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> AIVA กำลังพิมพ์เพื่อดึงรูปจากคลังความรู้...
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div ref={chatEndRef} />
                  </div>

                  {/* Message Input Box */}
                  <div className={`p-4 border-t relative ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'}`}>
                    {/* Slash Commands Popover */}
                    {showSlashCommands && chatMode === 'human' && (
                      <div className={`absolute bottom-full left-4 mb-2 w-72 rounded-2xl shadow-[0_10px_40px_-10px_rgba(0,0,0,0.15)] border overflow-hidden z-50 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'}`}>
                        <div className={`px-4 py-2.5 border-b text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900/50 border-slate-700 text-slate-400' : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-100 text-slate-500'}`}>
                          AIVA Slash Commands
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-2 space-y-1 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
                          <button onClick={() => insertSlashCommand('นี่คือตารางไซส์ของเรานะคะ คุณลูกค้าสามารถวัดเทียบได้เลยค่ะ ✨')} className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'hover:bg-indigo-50 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <div className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-white shadow-sm'}`}><FileText className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-500"/></div>
                            <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
                              <p className="font-bold">/ตารางไซส์</p>
                              <p className={`text-[9px] truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>ส่งภาพตารางไซส์และข้อความแนะนำ</p>
                            </div>
                          </button>
                          <button onClick={() => insertSlashCommand('โปรโมชั่นเดือนนี้ จัดส่งฟรีเมื่อซื้อครบ 2 ชิ้นค่ะ 🎉')} className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'hover:bg-indigo-50 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <div className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-white shadow-sm'}`}><Tag className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-500"/></div>
                            <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
                              <p className="font-bold">/โปรโมชั่น</p>
                              <p className={`text-[9px] truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>ส่งรายละเอียดโปรโมชั่นปัจจุบัน</p>
                            </div>
                          </button>
                          <button onClick={() => insertSlashCommand('รบกวนขอ ชื่อ-ที่อยู่ และเบอร์โทรศัพท์ สำหรับจัดส่งด้วยนะคะ 📦')} className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'hover:bg-indigo-50 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <div className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-white shadow-sm'}`}><MapPin className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-500"/></div>
                            <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
                              <p className="font-bold">/ขอที่อยู่</p>
                              <p className={`text-[9px] truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>ข้อความขอที่อยู่จัดส่ง</p>
                            </div>
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {chatMode === 'ai' ? (
                      <div className={`text-center p-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30' : 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300'}`}>
                        <p className={`text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-emerald-600'}`}><Bot className="w-4 h-4"/> AI กำลังดูแลการสนทนานี้ แอดมินสามารถดูได้อย่างเดียว</p>
                        <p className={`text-[10px] mt-1 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-emerald-500/70' : 'text-emerald-600/70'}`}>สลับเป็นโหมด 'แอดมินตอบเอง' ด้านบน หากต้องการพิมพ์ข้อความ</p>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <form onSubmit={handleSendAdminReply} className="flex gap-2 items-center relative">
                        <input 
                          type="file" 
                          ref={fileInputRef} 
                          onChange={handleAttachFile} 
                          accept="image/*"
                          className="hidden" 
                        />
                        <button 
                          type="button" 
                          onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                          className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-colors border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-600 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50 hover:border-indigo-200'}`}
                          title="แนบรูปภาพ/ไฟล์"
                        >
                          <Paperclip className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </button>
                        <button 
                          type="button" 
                          className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-colors border ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-600 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-500 hover:text-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-50 hover:border-pink-200'}`}
                          title="ส่งสินค้าจาก Catalog"
                        >
                          <ShoppingBag className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </button>
                        <input 
                          type="text" 
                          value={adminReplyText}
                          onChange={handleAdminTextChange}
                          placeholder="พิมพ์ / เพื่อใช้คีย์ลัด หรือพิมพ์ข้อความตอบกลับ..." 
                          className={`flex-1 border rounded-xl px-4 py-3.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-600 text-slate-200 placeholder:text-slate-500' : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-800'}`} 
                        />
                        <button type="submit" className={`px-5 py-3.5 rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-colors flex items-center justify-center ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500 hover:bg-indigo-400 text-white' : 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white'}`}><Send className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                      </form>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB: REPLY COMMENT */}
          {activeTab === 'replycomment' && (
            <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto h-full flex flex-col animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              {isProOrAbove ? (
                <>
                  <div className="mb-6 shrink-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>
                        <MessageCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" /> {t('replycomment')}
                      </h2>
                      <p className={`text-sm mt-1 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
                        ให้ AI ช่วยตอบคอมเมนต์ รีวิวอัตโนมัติ พร้อมฟีเจอร์วิเคราะห์อารมณ์และดึงลูกค้าเข้า Inbox ทันที
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => setShowReplySettings(true)} className="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors">
                      <Settings className="w-4 h-4"/> ตั้งค่าการตอบกลับ
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 shrink-0 mb-6">
                     <StatCard title="คอมเมนต์ที่ตอบแล้ว" value="3,420" icon={MessageSquare} color="blue" isDark={isDarkMode} />
                     <StatCard title="ดึงเข้า Inbox อัตโนมัติ" value="850" icon={Send} color="emerald" isDark={isDarkMode} />
                     <StatCard title="รีวิว 5 ดาวที่ตอบกลับ" value="845" icon={Star} color="amber" isDark={isDarkMode} />
                     <StatCard title="แจ้งเตือนฉุกเฉิน (โกรธ/1ดาว)" value="12" icon={AlertOctagon} color="rose" isDark={isDarkMode} />
                  </div>

                  <div className={`rounded-3xl border shadow-sm flex flex-col flex-1 overflow-hidden min-h-0 glass-card`}>
                    <div className={`p-5 border-b flex justify-between items-center shrink-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/50' : 'border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50'}`}>
                      <h3 className={`font-bold text-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>ประวัติการทำงานของ AI (Recent Activity)</h3>
                      <div className="flex gap-2">
                         <select 
                           value={replyPlatformFilter}
                           onChange={(e) => setReplyPlatformFilter(e.target.value)}
                           className={`text-xs border rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 outline-none font-bold shadow-sm ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}
                         >
                           <option value="All">{t('allChannels')}</option>
                           {connectedApps.includes('facebook') && <option value="Facebook">Facebook</option>}
                           {connectedApps.includes('instagram') && <option value="Instagram">Instagram</option>}
                           {connectedApps.includes('lazada') && <option value="Lazada">Lazada</option>}
                           {connectedApps.includes('tiktok') && <option value="TikTok Shop">TikTok Shop</option>}
                         </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-auto custom-scrollbar flex-1 p-0 bg-white/50 dark:bg-slate-900/50">
                      <table className="w-full text-left text-sm table-fixed min-w-[800px]">
                        <thead className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold border-b sticky top-0 z-10 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700' : 'bg-slate-50/90 backdrop-blur-md text-slate-500 border-slate-100'}`}>
                          <tr>
                            <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[15%]">แพลตฟอร์ม</th>
                            <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[35%]">ลูกค้า (อารมณ์ / ข้อความต้นทาง)</th>
                            <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[35%]">AI Action (การทำงาน)</th>
                            <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[15%] text-center">เวลา</th>
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody className={`divide-y ${isDarkMode ? 'divide-slate-700/50' : 'divide-slate-100'}`}>
                          {MOCK_REPLY_COMMENTS.filter(item => {
                            const platformId = Object.keys(PLATFORM_MAP).find(k => PLATFORM_MAP[k] === item.platform);
                            const isConnected = connectedApps.includes(platformId);
                            if (!isConnected && item.platform !== 'Facebook') return false; // Facebook as default fallback
                            return replyPlatformFilter === 'All' || item.platform === replyPlatformFilter;
                          }).map((item, i) => {
                            const IconCmp = item.icon;
                            return (
                            <tr key={i} className={`transition-colors ${item.isAlert ? (isDarkMode ? 'bg-rose-500/5 hover:bg-rose-500/10' : 'bg-rose-50/50 hover:bg-rose-50') : (isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700/30' : 'hover:bg-slate-50/50')}`}>
                              <td className="px-5 py-5">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                  <div className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-700' : 'bg-white shadow-sm border'} ${item.color}`}>
                                    <IconCmp className="w-4 h-4" />
                                  </div>
                                  <span className={`font-bold text-xs ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>{item.platform}</span>
                                </div>
                              </td>
                              <td className="px-5 py-5 overflow-hidden">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
                                  <span className={`font-bold text-xs ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{item.user}</span>
                                  <span className="text-sm" title="Sentiment">
                                    {item.sentiment === 'positive' && '😊'}
                                    {item.sentiment === 'neutral' && '😐'}
                                    {item.sentiment === 'negative' && '😡'}
                                  </span>
                                  {item.isReview && (
                                    <div className={`flex ${item.rating >= 4 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`}>
                                      {Array.from({ length: item.rating }).map((_, r) => <Star key={r} className="w-3 h-3 fill-current"/>)}
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                                <p className={`text-xs truncate ${item.isAlert ? 'text-rose-600 font-medium dark:text-rose-400' : (isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-600')}`}>"{item.comment}"</p>
                              </td>
                              <td className="px-5 py-5 overflow-hidden">
                                <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                                  <Bot className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${item.hidden ? 'text-slate-400' : item.isAlert ? 'text-rose-500' : 'text-indigo-500'}`} />
                                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                                    <p className={`text-xs leading-relaxed line-clamp-2 ${item.hidden ? 'text-slate-500 font-bold' : (isDarkMode ? 'text-indigo-200' : 'text-indigo-800')}`}>{item.aiReply}</p>
                                    <div className="flex">
                                      <span className={`text-[9px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${
                                        item.action === 'Comment-to-DM' ? 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 border-indigo-200' :
                                        item.action === 'Crisis Alert' ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 border-rose-200 animate-pulse' :
                                        item.action === 'Hide Spam' ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border-slate-200' :
                                        'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-200'
                                      }`}>
                                        ✓ {item.action}
                                      </span>
                                    </div>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              </td>
                              <td className="px-5 py-5 text-center text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">{item.time}</td>
                            </tr>
                          )})}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {showReplySettings && (
                    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                      <div className={`rounded-[2.5rem] w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden shadow-[0_20px_60px_-15px_rgba(0,0,0,0.3)] animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200 border-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white border-white'}`}>
                        <div className={`p-6 border-b flex justify-between items-center ${isDarkMode ? 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/80'}`}>
                          <h3 className={`font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-900'}`}>
                            <Settings className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-500" /> ตั้งค่า AIVA Reply & Social
                          </h3>
                          <button onClick={() => setShowReplySettings(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 p-1.5 rounded-full transition-colors shadow-sm"><X className="w-5 h-5"/></button>
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-6 space-y-5 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
                          
                          <div className="space-y-3">
                            <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700' : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-200'}`}>
                              <div>
                                <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>ตอบกลับคอมเมนต์ทั่วไป</p>
                                <p className={`text-[10px] mt-0.5 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>ตอบกลับโพสต์บน Facebook, IG อัตโนมัติ</p>
                              </div>
                              <div onClick={() => setReplySettings({...replySettings, autoReplyComments: !replySettings.autoReplyComments})} className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full relative cursor-pointer transition-colors ${replySettings.autoReplyComments ? 'bg-indigo-500' : (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-slate-300')}`}>
                                <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white absolute top-1 transition-transform ${replySettings.autoReplyComments ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-1'}`}></div>
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700' : 'bg-indigo-50/50 hover:bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-100 hover:border-indigo-300'}`}>
                              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5"><Send className="w-4 h-4"/></div>
                                <div>
                                  <p className={`text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400`}>Comment-to-Inbox (ดึงเข้าแชท)</p>
                                  <p className={`text-[10px] mt-0.5 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>หากลูกค้าถาม "ราคา, สนใจ, รายละเอียด" ให้ AI ตอบคอมเมนต์และทักแชท Inbox ทันทีเพื่อปิดการขาย</p>
                                </div>
                              </div>
                              <div onClick={() => setReplySettings({...replySettings, commentToInbox: !replySettings.commentToInbox})} className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full relative cursor-pointer transition-colors ${replySettings.commentToInbox ? 'bg-indigo-500' : (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-slate-300')}`}>
                                <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white absolute top-1 transition-transform ${replySettings.commentToInbox ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-1'}`}></div>
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700' : 'bg-rose-50/50 hover:bg-rose-50 border-rose-100 hover:border-rose-300'}`}>
                              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5"><AlertOctagon className="w-4 h-4"/></div>
                                <div>
                                  <p className={`text-sm font-bold text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400`}>Crisis Alert (แจ้งเตือนรีวิว 1 ดาว)</p>
                                  <p className={`text-[10px] mt-0.5 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>ให้ AI วิเคราะห์อารมณ์ลูกค้า หากพบความไม่พอใจ ให้ตอบขออภัยและแจ้งเตือนแอดมินด่วน</p>
                                </div>
                              </div>
                              <div onClick={() => setReplySettings({...replySettings, crisisAlert: !replySettings.crisisAlert})} className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full relative cursor-pointer transition-colors ${replySettings.crisisAlert ? 'bg-rose-500' : (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-slate-300')}`}>
                                <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white absolute top-1 transition-transform ${replySettings.crisisAlert ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-1'}`}></div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            
                            <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700' : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-white border-slate-200'}`}>
                              <div>
                                <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>ซ่อนคอมเมนต์สแปม/คำหยาบ</p>
                                <p className={`text-[10px] mt-0.5 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>ให้ AI กรองและซ่อนคอมเมนต์ที่ไม่เหมาะสมให้อัตโนมัติ</p>
                              </div>
                              <div onClick={() => setReplySettings({...replySettings, hideSpam: !replySettings.hideSpam})} className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full relative cursor-pointer transition-colors ${replySettings.hideSpam ? 'bg-slate-800' : (isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-600' : 'bg-slate-300')}`}>
                                <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white absolute top-1 transition-transform ${replySettings.hideSpam ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-1'}`}></div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                            <label className={`text-xs font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>คำสั่งเฉพาะเวลาตอบคอมเมนต์ (System Prompt)</label>
                            <textarea 
                              rows="3" 
                              value={replySettings.aiTone}
                              onChange={(e) => setReplySettings({...replySettings, aiTone: e.target.value})}
                              placeholder="เช่น ให้ AI ลงท้ายด้วยคำว่า 'นะคะ' เสมอ..."
                              className={`w-full border-2 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400 resize-none ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-800'}`}
                            />
                            <p className={`text-[10px] ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>AI จะพยายามใช้บริบทหรือลักษณะภาษาตามที่คุณพิมพ์ไว้ที่นี่เพื่อตอบกลับลูกค้าสาธารณะ</p>
                          </div>

                          <button onClick={() => setShowReplySettings(false)} className="w-full bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-2xl hover:bg-indigo-700 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/30 transform hover:-translate-y-1">
                            บันทึกการตั้งค่า
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </>
              ) : (
                <UpgradeOverlay requiredPlan="Pro" title="AI Reply Comment" icon={MessageCircle} description="ให้ AI ช่วยตอบคอมเมนต์บนโพสต์และรีวิวอัตโนมัติ เพื่อรักษา Engagement" />
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB: LEADS */}
          {activeTab === 'leads' && (
            <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto h-full flex flex-col animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              <div className="mb-6 shrink-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-end gap-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDarkMode ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>
                    <Users className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" /> {t('leads')}
                  </h2>
                  <p className={`text-sm mt-1 max-w-xl ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{t('leadDesc')}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => setShowAddLead(true)} className="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/20 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5"><Plus className="w-4 h-4"/> {t('addLead')}</button>
                  <button className={`px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border shadow-sm transition-colors ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-700' : 'bg-white text-indigo-600 border-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-50'}`}>{t('exportCsv')}</button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* KPI Cards for Leads */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 shrink-0 mb-6">
                 <StatCard title="Leads ทั้งหมด" value="1,248" icon={Users} color="indigo" isDark={isDarkMode} />
                 <StatCard title="คาดการณ์ยอดขาย (Est. Pipeline)" value="฿345,000" icon={TrendingUp} color="emerald" isDark={isDarkMode} />
                 <StatCard title="อัตราปิดการขาย (Win Rate)" value="24.5%" icon={CheckCircle2} color="rose" isDark={isDarkMode} />
              </div>

              <div className={`rounded-3xl flex flex-col flex-1 overflow-hidden border shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.04)] min-h-0 glass-card`}>
                <div className={`p-4 border-b flex justify-between items-center shrink-0 ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-700' : 'bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-100'}`}>
                  <div className="relative w-64">
                    <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                    <input type="text" placeholder={t('searchLead')} className={`text-sm rounded-xl pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20 border-2 transition-all ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-indigo-500' : 'bg-white border-slate-100 text-slate-800 focus:border-indigo-400'}`} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400' : 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700'}`}>
                      <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1" /> AI Insights Active
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="overflow-auto custom-scrollbar flex-1 p-0 bg-white/40 dark:bg-slate-900/40">
                  <table className="w-full text-left text-sm table-fixed min-w-[1000px]">
                    <thead className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold border-b sticky top-0 z-10 backdrop-blur-md ${isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800/90 text-slate-400 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white/90 text-slate-500 border-slate-100'}`}>
                      <tr>
                        <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[10%]">{t('captureDate')}</th>
                        <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[20%]">{t('customerName')}</th>
                        <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[20%]">{t('aiPersonaProfile')} <BrainCircuit className="w-3 h-3 inline ml-1 text-indigo-500"/></th>
                        <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[25%]">{t('intent')}</th>
                        <th className="px-5 py-4 w-[25%] text-right">{t('nextAction')} <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 inline ml-1 text-amber-500"/></th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className={`divide-y ${isDarkMode ? 'divide-slate-700/50' : 'divide-slate-100'}`}>
                      {leadsData.map((lead) => (
                        <tr key={lead.id} className={`transition-colors group ${isDarkMode ? 'hover:bg-slate-700/30' : 'hover:bg-indigo-50/30'}`}>
                          <td className={`px-5 py-4 text-xs font-medium ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{lead.date}</td>
                          <td className="px-5 py-4">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                              <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white shrink-0 shadow-sm ${
                                lead.platform === 'Line OA' ? 'bg-[#00B900]' : 
                                lead.platform === 'Facebook' ? 'bg-[#0084FF]' : 
                                lead.platform === 'Instagram' ? 'bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500 to-purple-600' : 
                                lead.platform === 'TikTok Shop' ? 'bg-slate-900' :
                                lead.platform === 'Lazada' ? 'bg-[#0F146D]' : 'bg-slate-800'
                              }`}>
                                {lead.name.charAt(0)}
                              </div>
                              <div className="overflow-hidden w-full">
                                <p className={`font-bold text-sm truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>{lead.name}</p>
                                <p className={`text-[10px] flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 truncate font-mono ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
                                  {lead.contact.includes('@') ? <Mail className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0"/> : lead.contact.includes('Direct') || lead.contact.includes('Inbox') ? <MessageSquare className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0"/> : <Phone className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0"/>} 
                                  {lead.contact}
                                </p>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-5 py-4">
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                              {lead.persona?.map((p, i) => (
                                <span key={i} className={`text-[9px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold border ${
                                  isDarkMode ? 'bg-slate-800 text-indigo-300 border-slate-700' : 'bg-white text-indigo-600 border-indigo-100 shadow-sm'
                                }`}>
                                  {p}
                                </span>
                              ))}
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-5 py-4">
                            <p className={`text-xs font-bold truncate ${isDarkMode ? 'text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>{lead.intent}</p>
                            <p className={`text-[10px] mt-1 font-bold ${isDarkMode ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-emerald-600'}`}>
                              {t('estValue')}: ฿{lead.estValue?.toLocaleString() || 0}
                            </p>
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
                            <button className={`w-full max-w-[140px] ml-auto py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold text-white shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all transform hover:-translate-y-0.5 ${lead.actionColor}`}>
                              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3"/> {lead.nextAction}
                            </button>
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
